## What changed

No new commits were authored. ## Details

- Base `origin/dev` was fetched and verified (`68447eea`) immediately before the push.

## Tested

<details>
<summary>R01 — combined-1F1B / NCCL-EP interface</summary>

TransformerLayerNode still reads config.moe_ncclep_zero_copy and passes ncclep_zero_copy to ScheduleNode, but the final configuration and ScheduleNode signature no longer define them. The NCCL-EP zero-copy implementation from #5735 is also absent.

Related PRs: [#5735](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/5735). PR authors: `YangFei1990`.

Code: [megatron/core/models/common/utils.py:255](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/bbe7bbaa0f871fa343df2f9e67e37a75241b2c5d/megatron/core/models/common/utils.py#L255), [megatron/core/pipeline_parallel/utils.py:150](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/bbe7bbaa0f871fa343df2f9e67e37a75241b2c5d/megatron/core/pipeline_parallel/utils.py#L150), [megatron/core/transformer/moe/token_dispatcher.py:1693](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/bbe7bbaa0f871fa343df2f9e67e37a75241b2c5d/megatron/core/transformer/moe/token_dispatcher.py#L1693).

Evidence / limits: The config read is unconditional when TransformerLayerNode is constructed, including non-NCCL dispatchers. This is a concrete caller/config/callee mismatch.

</details>

Evidence / limits: The guard was added by main #4967, not dev #5470. With overlap_grad_reduce=True, reduce_scatter_with_fp32_accumulation=True and a compact non-DistOpt buffer, grad_reduce_handle is initialized to None and the all-reduce branch does not set it, but the final assertion requires it to be non-None.

<details>
<summary>Other inspected differences with no additional finding established</summary>

- GDN deterministic torch kernel was moved from gdn.py to common.py; scale/cp_context and public exports are retained.
- GDN runtime CP, chunkwise CP, pre-GDR fusion, recompute and KDA integration remain; the #5843/#6088 package split itself is present.
- MTP context fixed buffer, GPT/Hybrid producers, and controller consumer consistently use the context-based hidden-state path.
- Mamba prefix matching moved from the engine into the context and is constrained to the scheduled chunk; it is not simply missing.
- mamba_reshard.py was replaced by ssm_reshard.py and the new transfer backends.
- RL conflicts combine the new multi-turn/placeholder handling with existing logging; no additional actionable source mismatch was established.
- Checkpoint quote/format-only conflicts and the retained GPT-to-Hybrid translation are not findings.
- Teacher checkpoint loading has an implementation in megatron/post_training/checkpointing.py; its removal from training/checkpointing.py is not listed as a lost feature.
- Generalized Tensor Parallelism is only evaluated within the selected 100-commit range; later main changes are not treated as missing.

</details>
